```bash
diff --git a/src/app/screens/RoutinesScreen.tsx b/src/app/screens/RoutinesScreen.tsx
index 0dfb29a..27a42df 100644
--- a/src/app/screens/RoutinesScreen.tsx
+++ b/src/app/screens/RoutinesScreen.tsx
@@ -1,12 +1,88 @@
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
+import { ConfirmDialog } from '../components/ConfirmDialog.tsx';
+import { Icon } from '../components/Icon.tsx';
+import { danceName } from '../i18n/index.ts';
+import { unlockAudioInsideTap } from '../platform/audio-context.ts';
+import type { UiState } from '../router/routes.ts';
+import { ROUTINES_MAX } from '../routines/limits.ts';
+import { routinePlayability, type Routine } from '../routines/routine.ts';
+import { requestAutoplay } from '../state/autoplay.ts';
 import { useI18n } from '../state/i18n.ts';
+import { navigate } from '../state/route.ts';
+import { useRoutines } from '../state/routines.ts';
 
-// S3 の器。一覧と「＋ 新しいルーティン」は段3。
-export function RoutinesScreen() {
+// S3 ルーティン一覧（仕様 §4・Ruling 35・40・41・44）。上から「＋ 新しいルーティン」→ 一覧（作った順）。
+// 行をタップで開く（同時に1つ）。開いた行: 鳴らせる時は ▶ 再生（S5 で即再生）・編集・削除。
+// 「配る」は段5で足す（Ruling 41）。ui-state=confirm-delete は最初の行の削除確認を見た目だけ出す（Ruling 43）。
+export function RoutinesScreen({ uiState }: { uiState?: UiState }) {
   const { translator } = useI18n();
+  const { routines, remove } = useRoutines();
+  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(null);
+  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState<Routine | null>(null);
+  const locale = translator.locale;
+  useEffect(() => { if (uiState === 'confirm-delete' && routines[0]) setDeleting(routines[0]); }, [uiState]);
+
+  // ▶: このタップの中で音を解除し、一回限りの印を付けて S5 へ（Ruling 32・40）。
+  const play = (routine: Routine) => {
+    try { unlockAudioInsideTap(); } catch { /* 音が使えない端末は S5 の2秒判定で案内する */ }
+    requestAutoplay({ kind: 'routine', id: routine.id });
+    navigate({ screen: 'S5', params: { routineId: routine.id } });
+  };
+
   return (
     <main class="dc-screen" data-screen="S3">
       <h1 class="dc-heading">{translator.t('routinesTitle')}</h1>
-      <p data-body>{translator.t('routinesEmpty')}</p>
+      <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-primary dc-wide" disabled={routines.length >= ROUTINES_MAX}
+        onClick={() => navigate({ screen: 'S4', params: {} })}>
+        <Icon name="plus" /><span>{translator.t('newRoutine')}</span>
+      </button>
+      {routines.length === 0
+        ? <p class="dc-empty" data-body>{translator.t('routinesEmpty')}</p>
+        : (
+          <div class="dc-list" role="list" data-list>
+            {routines.map(routine => {
+              const open = routine.id === openId;
+              const playability = routinePlayability(routine);
+              const dance = danceName(routine.danceId, locale);
+              const count = routine.figures.length;
+              const summary = count === 1 ? translator.t('routineSummaryOne', { dance }) : translator.t('routineSummary', { dance, count });
+              return (
+                <div key={routine.id} role="listitem" class="dc-row" data-row data-open={open ? '' : undefined}>
+                  <button type="button" class="dc-row-head" aria-expanded={open ? 'true' : 'false'} onClick={() => setOpenId(open ? null : routine.id)}>
+                    <span class="dc-row-text">
+                      <b>{routine.name}</b>
+                      <small>{summary}{routine.receivedFrom ? ` · ${translator.t('receivedFrom', { name: routine.receivedFrom.distributor })}` : ''}</small>
+                    </span>
+                    <Icon name="chevron" class="dc-row-chev" />
+                  </button>
+                  {open && (
+                    <div class="dc-row-body" data-row-body>
+                      {playability !== 'ok' && <p class="dc-row-note">{translator.t(playability === 'empty' ? 'needsFigures' : 'needsFix')}</p>}
+                      <div class="dc-row-actions" data-group="routine-actions">
+                        {playability === 'ok' && (
+                          <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-primary dc-wide" onClick={() => play(routine)}>
+                            <Icon name="play" /><span>{translator.t('play')}</span>
+                          </button>
+                        )}
+                        <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary" onClick={() => navigate({ screen: 'S4', params: { routineId: routine.id } })}>
+                          <Icon name="edit" /><span>{translator.t('edit')}</span>
+                        </button>
+                        <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary" onClick={() => setDeleting(routine)}>
+                          <Icon name="trash" /><span>{translator.t('delete')}</span>
+                        </button>
+                      </div>
+                    </div>
+                  )}
+                </div>
+              );
+            })}
+          </div>
+        )}
+      {deleting && (
+        <ConfirmDialog title={translator.t('confirmDeleteTitle')} detail={deleting.name} confirmLabel={translator.t('confirmDelete')}
+          onCancel={() => setDeleting(null)}
+          onConfirm={() => { void remove(deleting.id); setDeleting(null); setOpenId(null); }} />
+      )}
     </main>
   );
 }
PATCH
```

```bash
+.dc-button { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 8px; min-height: 48px; min-width: 48px; padding: 0 14px;
+  border: var(--bw) solid var(--primary); border-radius: var(--r-s); background: var(--surface); color: var(--primary); font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }

+.dc-row-chev { color: var(--ink2); }

+.dc-row[data-open] .dc-row-chev { transform: rotate(180deg); }
+.dc-row-body { padding: 8px 10px 12px; }
+.dc-row-note { margin: 0 0 12px; font-size: 18px; color: var(--ink2); }
+.dc-row-actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; }
+.dc-row-actions .dc-button--secondary { flex: 1; }
+.dc-dialog { width: min(100% - 32px, 420px); padding: 20px 16px 16px; border: none; border-radius: var(--r); background: var(--surface); color: var(--ink); }
+.dc-dialog::backdrop { background: rgba(20, 33, 61, 0.55); }
+.dc-dialog-title { margin: 0 0 4px; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 700; }
+.dc-dialog-detail { margin: 0 0 16px; font-size: 18px; color: var(--ink2); overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
+.dc-dialog-buttons { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 12px; }
+.dc-dialog-buttons .dc-button { min-height: 56px; }
+

   .dc-tabs { top: 0; bottom: auto; height: var(--tabh-top); justify-content: center; padding: 8px 24px; border-top: none; border-bottom: var(--bw) solid var(--line); }
```

### Task 9: S4 ルーティン編集（ドラッグの並べ替えつき）

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/routines/reorder-drag.ts`・`test/app/routines/reorder-drag.test.ts`（ドラッグの判断。Ruling 49）
- Create: `src/app/platform/viewport.ts`（見えている高さ・ページのスクロール・毎フレーム・中断の知らせ。`window`・`requestAnimationFrame` はここだけ）
- Create: `src/app/routines/useReorderDrag.ts`（Pointer Events のフック）
- Modify: `src/app/components/icons.svg`・`icon-names.ts`・`Icon.tsx`（`grip`。play・stop と同じ塗り）
- Modify: `src/app/i18n/messages.ts`（S4 の文言・`move`・`moveFigureLabel`）
- Create: `src/app/routines/FigureForm.tsx`（開いた行の入力: 名前と候補・カウントと理由の1行・よく使うカウント・決定／追加する）
- Create: `src/app/screens/RoutineEditScreen.tsx`
- Modify: `src/app/App.tsx`（S4 を描く。**`key` を付けない**: 新規 → 編集のハッシュの置き換えで作り直さないため）
- Modify: `src/app/styles/base.css`（S4・つかみ・線・`--now-bg`）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RoutineEditor`（`move` 込み。Task 7）、`makeId`・`createRoutine`・`uniqueRoutineName`・`ROUTINE_NAME_MAX`・`allowedSymbols`・`figureNameSuggestions`・`hasFigureNameSuggestions`（Task 2）、`useRoutines`（Task 3）、`replaceRoute`（Task 4）、`routineBaseBpm`（Task 5）、`usePracticeSession`・`MoreOptions`・`NoticeBox`（Task 6）、エンジンの `figureDisplayName`・`dropDestination`
- Produces:
  - `DRAG_START_PX=8`・`AUTO_SCROLL_EDGE_PX=48`・`AUTO_SCROLL_STEP_PX=8`・`isDragStart(dx, dy)`・`dropTargetFromY(rows, y): { targetIndex; placeAfter } | null`・`autoScrollStep(y, visibleTop, visibleBottom)`
  - `useReorderDrag({ list, count, onStart, onDrop, onTap, onStep }): { gripProps(index); view: { from; lineTop } | null }`
  - S4 の DOM（`[data-screen="S4"]`、0件の主ボタンと「全体を試聴」は `.dc-dock` の `.dc-primary`、フィガーの行は `.dc-row[data-index]` の中の `.dc-row-line` に見出し `.dc-figure-head` とつかみ `.dc-grip`（2件以上の時）、開いた行の入力は `.dc-figure-form`（`[data-group="figure-form"]`）、行の操作は `[data-group="figure-actions"]`、不正なカウントは `.dc-input--bad[aria-invalid="true"]` と `.dc-reason`、末尾の追加は `.dc-add-tail`、ドラッグ中は `.dc-row--dragging` と `.dc-drop-line`）

`test/app/routines/reorder-drag.test.ts`（新規・全文）:
```ts
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { dropDestination } from '../../../src/engine/routine-editor-order.js';
import { AUTO_SCROLL_STEP_PX, autoScrollStep, dropTargetFromY, isDragStart } from '../../../src/app/routines/reorder-drag.ts';

test('a drag starts at 8px from the press point; less is a tap (Ruling 49-4)', () => {
  assert.equal(isDragStart(7.9, 0), false);
  assert.equal(isDragStart(0, 8), true);
  assert.equal(isDragStart(5, 6), false);  // 7.81
  assert.equal(isDragStart(6, 6), true);   // 8.49
});

const ROWS = Array.from({ length: 13 }, (_, index) => ({ top: 100 + index * 65, bottom: 165 + index * 65 }));

test('the finger height picks the row and the side of it', () => {
  assert.equal(dropTargetFromY([], 10), null);
  assert.deepEqual(dropTargetFromY(ROWS, 50), { targetIndex: 0, placeAfter: false });     // 先頭より上
  assert.deepEqual(dropTargetFromY(ROWS, 2000), { targetIndex: 12, placeAfter: true });   // 末尾より下
  assert.deepEqual(dropTargetFromY(ROWS, 110), { targetIndex: 0, placeAfter: false });
  assert.deepEqual(dropTargetFromY(ROWS, 132.5), { targetIndex: 0, placeAfter: true });   // 中央ちょうどは後
  assert.deepEqual(dropTargetFromY(ROWS, 165), { targetIndex: 1, placeAfter: false });    // 境目は次の行の上側
});

test('together with dropDestination: moves land where the line shows (13 figures)', () => {
  const to = (from: number, y: number) => { const target = dropTargetFromY(ROWS, y)!; return dropDestination(from, target.targetIndex, target.placeAfter, 13); };
  assert.equal(to(2, ROWS[9]!.top + 40), 9);     // 2 → 9 の行の下半分
  assert.equal(to(9, ROWS[2]!.top + 10), 2);     // 9 → 2 の行の上半分
  assert.equal(to(0, 2000), 12);                 // 先頭 → 末尾
  assert.equal(to(12, 0), 0);                    // 末尾 → 先頭
  assert.equal(to(5, ROWS[5]!.top + 10), 5);     // 同じ位置
});

test('auto-scroll only near the edges of the visible area, at a fixed step', () => {
  assert.equal(autoScrollStep(30, 0, 570), -AUTO_SCROLL_STEP_PX);
  assert.equal(autoScrollStep(300, 0, 570), 0);
  assert.equal(autoScrollStep(560, 0, 570), AUTO_SCROLL_STEP_PX);
  assert.equal(autoScrollStep(48, 0, 570), 0);
});
```

`src/app/routines/reorder-drag.ts`（新規・全文）:
```ts
// フィガーのドラッグの判断（Ruling 49）。画面（useReorderDrag）から座標を渡し、ここは数だけで決める。
// 行き先の番号は既存の dropDestination（エンジン）で求める。
export const DRAG_START_PX = 8;          // 押した点からこれ以上動いたらドラッグ。未満で離したらタップ（行を開く）
export const AUTO_SCROLL_EDGE_PX = 48;   // 見えている領域の上下端からこの内側に指が入ったら自動スクロール
export const AUTO_SCROLL_STEP_PX = 8;    // 1フレームに動かす量（固定速度。指の位置で加速しない）

export interface RowBox { top: number; bottom: number }
export interface DropTarget { targetIndex: number; placeAfter: boolean }

export function isDragStart(dx: number, dy: number): boolean {
  return Math.hypot(dx, dy) >= DRAG_START_PX;
}

// 指の高さ y から「どの行の前か後か」。最初の行より上は先頭の前、最後の行より下は末尾の後。行の中央ちょうどは後。
export function dropTargetFromY(rows: readonly RowBox[], y: number): DropTarget | null {
  if (rows.length === 0) return null;
  const last = rows.length - 1;
  if (y < rows[0]!.top) return { targetIndex: 0, placeAfter: false };
  if (y >= rows[last]!.bottom) return { targetIndex: last, placeAfter: true };
  const index = rows.findIndex(row => y < row.bottom);
  const row = rows[index]!;
  return { targetIndex: index, placeAfter: y >= row.top + (row.bottom - row.top) / 2 };
}

// 自動スクロールの向き（-1 上へ・1 下へ・0 止める）×量。visibleTop/Bottom は固定の帯を除いた見えている領域。
export function autoScrollStep(y: number, visibleTop: number, visibleBottom: number): number {
  if (y < visibleTop + AUTO_SCROLL_EDGE_PX) return -AUTO_SCROLL_STEP_PX;
  if (y > visibleBottom - AUTO_SCROLL_EDGE_PX) return AUTO_SCROLL_STEP_PX;
  return 0;
}
```

`src/app/platform/viewport.ts`（新規・全文）:
```ts
// ドラッグ（Ruling 49）が触る画面の面はここだけ: 見えている高さ・ページのスクロール・毎フレーム・中断の知らせ。
export function viewportHeight(): number {
  return window.innerHeight;
}

export function scrollPageBy(dy: number): void {
  window.scrollBy(0, dy);
}

// 次の描画の前に1回。戻り値で取り消し。
export function nextFrame(callback: () => void): () => void {
  const id = requestAnimationFrame(callback);
  return () => cancelAnimationFrame(id);
}

// ドラッグを捨てる合図: 画面がフォーカスを失う・裏に回る・Esc。戻り値で解除。
export function onDragInterrupt(listener: () => void): () => void {
  const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => { if (event.key === 'Escape') listener(); };
  const onHidden = () => { if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') listener(); };
  window.addEventListener('blur', listener);
  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onHidden);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('blur', listener);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onHidden);
  };
}
```

`src/app/routines/useReorderDrag.ts`（新規・全文）:
```ts
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { dropDestination } from '../../engine/routine-editor-order.js';
import { nextFrame, onDragInterrupt, scrollPageBy, viewportHeight } from '../platform/viewport.ts';
import { autoScrollStep, dropTargetFromY, isDragStart, type DropTarget } from './reorder-drag.ts';

// S4 のフィガーのドラッグ（Ruling 49）。Pointer Events だけ（マウス・タッチ・ペン共通。HTML5 drag は iOS のタッチで来ない）。
// つかみ（.dc-grip）を押して 8px 以上動いたらドラッグ、未満で離したら click＝タップ（行を開く）。
// 行き先は一覧の行（.dc-row[data-index]）の矩形と指の高さから毎フレーム決める（自動スクロール中も更新）。
export interface ReorderDragOptions {
  list: { current: HTMLElement | null };
  count: number;
  onStart(index: number): void;          // 開いている行を閉じる・試聴を止める
  onDrop(from: number, to: number): void;
  onTap(index: number): void;
  onStep(index: number, delta: -1 | 1): void;   // つかみの上下矢印
}

interface Press { index: number; pointerId: number; x: number; y: number; lastY: number; dragging: boolean }
export interface DragView { from: number; lineTop: number | null }

export function useReorderDrag(options: ReorderDragOptions) {
  const latest = useRef(options);
  latest.current = options;
  const press = useRef<Press | null>(null);
  const target = useRef<DropTarget | null>(null);
  const stopLoop = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null);
  const ignoreClick = useRef(false);
  const [view, setView] = useState<DragView | null>(null);

  const rows = () => [...(latest.current.list.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.dc-row[data-index]') ?? [])];

  // いまの指の高さから行き先と線の位置を決める。
  const locate = () => {
    const current = press.current;
    const list = latest.current.list.current;
    if (!current || !list) return;
    const boxes = rows().map(row => row.getBoundingClientRect());
    target.current = dropTargetFromY(boxes, current.lastY);
    const box = target.current ? boxes[target.current.targetIndex]! : null;
    const lineTop = box && target.current ? (target.current.placeAfter ? box.bottom : box.top) - list.getBoundingClientRect().top : null;
    setView({ from: current.index, lineTop });
  };

  // 毎フレーム: 端に近ければ画面を動かし、行き先を更新する。
  const loop = () => {
    if (!press.current?.dragging) return;
    const dock = latest.current.list.current?.ownerDocument.querySelector('.dc-dock');
    const visibleBottom = dock ? dock.getBoundingClientRect().top : viewportHeight();
    const step = autoScrollStep(press.current.lastY, 0, visibleBottom);
    if (step !== 0) scrollPageBy(step);
    locate();
    stopLoop.current = nextFrame(loop);
  };

  const finish = () => {
    stopLoop.current?.();
    stopLoop.current = null;
    press.current = null;
    target.current = null;
    setView(null);
  };

  useEffect(() => onDragInterrupt(() => { if (press.current) finish(); }), []);
  useEffect(() => () => stopLoop.current?.(), []);

  const gripProps = (index: number) => ({
    onPointerDown(event: PointerEvent) {
      if (event.button > 0) return;
      event.preventDefault();   // iOS の文字選択・長押しメニューを出さない
      (event.currentTarget as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
      ignoreClick.current = false;
      press.current = { index, pointerId: event.pointerId, x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, lastY: event.clientY, dragging: false };
    },
    onPointerMove(event: PointerEvent) {
      const current = press.current;
      if (!current || event.pointerId !== current.pointerId) return;
      current.lastY = event.clientY;
      if (!current.dragging) {
        if (!isDragStart(event.clientX - current.x, event.clientY - current.y)) return;
        current.dragging = true;
        latest.current.onStart(current.index);
        stopLoop.current = nextFrame(loop);
      }
      locate();
    },
    onPointerUp(event: PointerEvent) {
      const current = press.current;
      if (!current || event.pointerId !== current.pointerId) return;
      if (!current.dragging) { press.current = null; return; }   // 動かしていない: 続く click がタップになる
      ignoreClick.current = true;
      const drop = target.current;
      finish();
      if (drop) latest.current.onDrop(current.index, dropDestination(current.index, drop.targetIndex, drop.placeAfter, latest.current.count));
    },
    onPointerCancel() { finish(); },
    onClick() {
      if (ignoreClick.current) { ignoreClick.current = false; return; }
      latest.current.onTap(index);   // 軽く押した・Enter・Space
    },
    onKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (event.key !== 'ArrowUp' && event.key !== 'ArrowDown') return;
      event.preventDefault();
      latest.current.onStep(index, event.key === 'ArrowUp' ? -1 : 1);
    },
    onContextMenu(event: Event) { event.preventDefault(); }
  });

  return { gripProps, view };
}
```

`src/app/routines/FigureForm.tsx`（新規・全文）:
```tsx
import { DANCE_PROFILES } from '../../engine/dance-profiles.js';
import { Icon } from '../components/Icon.tsx';
import type { MessageKey } from '../i18n/index.ts';
import { formatCounts } from '../lib/count-display.ts';
import { useI18n } from '../state/i18n.ts';
import { allowedSymbols } from './counts.ts';
import type { DraftCheck, DraftReason, FigureDraft } from './editor.ts';
import { figureNameSuggestions, hasFigureNameSuggestions } from './figure-names.ts';
import { COUNTS_MAX, FIGURE_NAME_MAX } from './limits.ts';

// 開いた行の入力（Ruling 36-3・38・39）。名前（候補は verified 名だけ）・カウント（種目のよく使うカウント）・決定。
// 全部で1つの [data-group="figure-form"]（名前・カウント・候補5・よく使う2・決定＝最大10で U5 に収まる）。
const REASON_TEXT: Record<Exclude<DraftReason, null>, MessageKey> = {
  symbols: 'allowedSymbols', leading: 'startWithBeat', invalid: 'countsInvalid', name: 'nameNeeded'
};
const TEMPLATES = DANCE_PROFILES as Record<string, { templates: readonly string[] }>;

export function FigureForm({ idPrefix, danceId, draft, check, adding, onDraft, onDecide }: {
  idPrefix: string; danceId: string; draft: FigureDraft; check: DraftCheck; adding: boolean;
  onDraft(patch: Partial<FigureDraft>): void; onDecide(): void;
}) {
  const { translator } = useI18n();
  const suggestions = hasFigureNameSuggestions(danceId) ? figureNameSuggestions(danceId, draft.name, translator.locale) : [];
  const countsBad = check.reason === 'symbols' || check.reason === 'leading' || check.reason === 'invalid';
  const reasonId = `${idPrefix}-reason`;
  return (
    <div class="dc-figure-form" data-group="figure-form">
      <div class="dc-field">
        <div class="dc-field-label" id={`${idPrefix}-name-label`}>{translator.t('figureName')}</div>
        <input class="dc-input" type="text" aria-labelledby={`${idPrefix}-name-label`} value={draft.name} maxLength={FIGURE_NAME_MAX}
          autocomplete="off" onInput={event => onDraft({ name: (event.currentTarget as HTMLInputElement).value })} />
        {suggestions.length > 0 && (
          <div class="dc-suggestions" role="group" aria-label={translator.t('figureSuggestions')}>
            {suggestions.map(name => <button key={name} type="button" class="dc-suggestion" onClick={() => onDraft({ name })}>{name}</button>)}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <div class="dc-field">
        <div class="dc-field-label" id={`${idPrefix}-counts-label`}>{translator.t('counts')}</div>
        <input class={`dc-input dc-input--counts${countsBad ? ' dc-input--bad' : ''}`} type="text" aria-labelledby={`${idPrefix}-counts-label`}
          aria-invalid={countsBad ? 'true' : undefined} aria-describedby={check.reason ? reasonId : undefined}
          value={draft.counts} maxLength={COUNTS_MAX} autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" spellcheck={false}
          onInput={event => onDraft({ counts: (event.currentTarget as HTMLInputElement).value })}
          onKeyDown={event => { if (event.key === 'Enter' && check.valid) onDecide(); }} />
        {check.reason && (
          <div class="dc-reason" id={reasonId}>
            {check.reason !== 'name' && <Icon name="alert" />}
            <span>{translator.t(REASON_TEXT[check.reason], { symbols: allowedSymbols(danceId) })}</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <div class="dc-field">
        <div class="dc-field-label">{translator.t('commonCounts')}</div>
        <div class="dc-chips">
          {TEMPLATES[danceId]!.templates.map(counts => (
            <button key={counts} type="button" class="dc-chip" onClick={() => onDraft({ counts })}>{formatCounts(danceId, counts)}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-primary dc-wide" disabled={!check.valid} onClick={onDecide}>
        <Icon name={adding ? 'plus' : 'check'} /><span>{translator.t(adding ? 'add' : 'done')}</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useReducer, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { DANCE_PROFILES, getDanceProfile } from '../../engine/dance-profiles.js';
import { figureDisplayName } from '../../engine/figure-terminology.js';
import { BackButton } from '../components/BackButton.tsx';
import { Icon } from '../components/Icon.tsx';
import { danceName } from '../i18n/index.ts';
import { formatCounts } from '../lib/count-display.ts';
import { MoreOptions, NoticeBox, type AlertNotice } from '../practice/controls.tsx';
import { routineBaseBpm } from '../practice/routine-preferences.ts';
import { BPM_MAX, BPM_MIN, clampBpm, type SpeedProfile } from '../practice/speed.ts';
import { usePracticeSession } from '../practice/use-session.ts';
import { RoutineEditor } from '../routines/editor.ts';
import { useReorderDrag } from '../routines/useReorderDrag.ts';
import { FigureForm } from '../routines/FigureForm.tsx';
import { makeId } from '../routines/ids.ts';
import { ROUTINE_NAME_MAX } from '../routines/limits.ts';
import { createRoutine, uniqueRoutineName, type Routine } from '../routines/routine.ts';
import { useI18n } from '../state/i18n.ts';
import { replaceRoute } from '../state/route.ts';
import { useRoutines } from '../state/routines.ts';
import { useSettings } from '../state/settings.ts';

const DANCE_IDS = Object.keys(DANCE_PROFILES);
const TEMPLATES = DANCE_PROFILES as Record<string, { templates: readonly string[] }>;

// S4 ルーティン編集（仕様 §4・Ruling 36〜39）。新規はまず種目を選ぶ（Ruling 37）。
// 変更は確定のたびに自動保存（保存ボタンなし）。最初の保存で ID を発行し、ハッシュを編集の形に置き換える。
export function RoutineEditScreen({ routineId }: { routineId?: string }) {
  const { translator } = useI18n();
  const { routines, save } = useRoutines();
  const { settings } = useSettings();
  const locale = translator.locale;
  const [, rerender] = useReducer((count: number) => count + 1, 0);
  const editor = useRef<RoutineEditor | null>(null);
  const saved = useRef(false);   // 端末に1回でも保存したか（新規は最初の確定まで false）
  const [saveFailed, setSaveFailed] = useState(false);
  const [choosingDance, setChoosingDance] = useState(false);   // フィガー0件の時の「変える」
  const { session, view } = usePracticeSession('S4');

  // 同じ画面のままハッシュだけ替わった（ブラウザの戻る・進む）。自分で置き換えた時（新規 → 編集）は編集を続け、
  // それ以外は、開いている行を原則どおり閉じて保存してから、新しいハッシュのルーティンで開き直す。
  const shownFor = useRef(routineId);
  if (routineId !== shownFor.current) {
    shownFor.current = routineId;
    if (!(routineId && editor.current?.routine.id === routineId)) {
      if (editor.current?.close() && saved.current) void save({ ...editor.current.routine, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
      editor.current = null;
      saved.current = false;
    }
  }
  if (routineId && editor.current?.routine.id !== routineId) {
    const routine = routines.find(item => item.id === routineId);
    if (routine) {
      editor.current = new RoutineEditor(routine, () => makeId('f'));
      saved.current = true;
    }
  }

  const defaultName = (danceId: string, others: readonly Routine[]) =>
    uniqueRoutineName(translator.t('defaultRoutineName', { dance: danceName(danceId, locale) }), others.map(item => item.name));

  const persist = () => {
    const current = editor.current;
    if (!current) return;
    const routine = { ...current.routine, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
    void save(routine).then(ok => setSaveFailed(!ok));
    if (!saved.current) {
      saved.current = true;
      replaceRoute({ screen: 'S4', params: { routineId: routine.id } });
    }
  };

  // 操作の共通の入口: 試聴を止めて、変えて、変わったら保存して、描き直す。
  const act = (change: (current: RoutineEditor) => boolean) => {
    const current = editor.current;
    if (!current) return;
    session.stop();
    if (change(current)) persist();
    rerender(0);
  };

  // ドラッグで並べ替え（Ruling 49）。始めたら試聴を止め、開いている行を原則どおり閉じる。落としたら1回の変更。
  const list = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const focusGrip = (index: number) => setTimeout(() => list.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.dc-grip')[index]?.focus({ preventScroll: true }), 0);
  const drag = useReorderDrag({
    list,
    count: editor.current?.routine.figures.length ?? 0,
    onStart: () => act(current => current.close()),
    onDrop: (from, to) => { act(current => current.move(from, to)); focusGrip(to); },
    onTap: index => act(current => (current.open?.kind === 'edit' && current.open.index === index ? current.close() : current.openRow(index))),
    onStep: (index, delta) => {
      const to = index + delta;
      if (to < 0 || to >= (editor.current?.routine.figures.length ?? 0)) return;
      act(current => current.move(index, to));
      focusGrip(to);
    }
  });

  // 戻る（端末の戻る・タブ移動も）: 開いている行は原則どおり閉じて（有効なら確定）保存する（Ruling 36-1）。
  useEffect(() => () => {
    const current = editor.current;
    if (current && current.close()) persist();
  }, []);

  // 種目を選ぶ（新規）・選び直す（フィガー0件の「変える」）。選び直しで、名前が前の種目の既定のままなら新しい種目の既定に。
  // 種目を選んだだけでは保存しない（Ruling 37: 最初の確定は名前・フィガー・くわしくの値）。保存済みなら保存し直す。
  const pickDance = (danceId: string) => {
    const existing = editor.current;
    setChoosingDance(false);
    if (!existing) {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      editor.current = new RoutineEditor(createRoutine({ id: makeId('r'), danceId, name: defaultName(danceId, routines), now }), () => makeId('f'));
      rerender(0);
      return;
    }
    const others = routines.filter(item => item.id !== existing.routine.id);
    const keepName = existing.routine.name !== defaultName(existing.routine.danceId, others);
    if (existing.changeDance(danceId, keepName ? existing.routine.name : defaultName(danceId, others)) && saved.current) persist();
    rerender(0);
  };

  const current = editor.current;
  if (!current || choosingDance) {
    return (
      <main class="dc-screen dc-screen--focus" data-screen="S4">
        <header class="dc-focus-head"><BackButton screen="S4" /><span class="dc-focus-title">{translator.t(current ? 'editRoutineTitle' : 'newRoutineTitle')}</span></header>
        <h1 class="dc-heading">{translator.t('chooseDance')}</h1>
        <div class="dc-tiles" role="group" aria-label={translator.t('danceGroup')} data-group="dance">
          {DANCE_IDS.map(danceId => (
            <button key={danceId} type="button" class="dc-tile" onClick={() => pickDance(danceId)}>
              <b>{danceName(danceId, locale)}</b>
              <small>{formatCounts(danceId, TEMPLATES[danceId]!.templates[0]!)}</small>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const routine = current.routine;
  const profile = getDanceProfile(routine.danceId) as SpeedProfile & { id: string };
  const open = current.open;
  const count = routine.figures.length;
  const playing = view.playing || view.starting;
  const notice: AlertNotice = saveFailed ? 'save-failed' : view.notice;
  const sounding = (index: number) => view.playing && view.figureIndex === index;

  const changeDance = () => {
    session.stop();
    setChoosingDance(true);
  };

  const previewOpen = () => {
    const preview = current.previewOpen();
    if (!preview) return;
    if (playing) { session.stop(); return; }
    void session.play({
      kind: 'routine', danceId: routine.danceId, figures: preview.figures, range: { startIndex: preview.index, endIndex: preview.index },
      bpm: routineBaseBpm(profile, routine), metronome: settings.metronome, countInBars: 0, loop: false, track: null
    });
  };

  const previewAll = () => {
    if (playing) { session.stop(); return; }
    if (current.close()) persist();
    rerender(0);
    void session.play({
      kind: 'routine', danceId: routine.danceId, figures: current.routine.figures,
      bpm: routineBaseBpm(profile, routine), metronome: settings.metronome, countInBars: routine.countInBars, loop: false, track: null
    });
  };

  return (
    <main class="dc-screen dc-screen--focus dc-screen--dock" data-screen="S4" data-playing={playing ? 'true' : 'false'}>
      <header class="dc-focus-head"><BackButton screen="S4" /><span class="dc-focus-title">{translator.t('editRoutineTitle')}</span></header>
      <NoticeBox notice={notice} onClose={() => session.dismissNotice()} />
      <RoutineNameField name={routine.name} onCommit={name => act(editor => editor.rename(name))} />
      <div class="dc-dance-line">
        <span>{translator.t('danceLine', { dance: danceName(routine.danceId, locale) })}</span>
        {count === 0 && <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary" onClick={changeDance}>{translator.t('changeDance')}</button>}
      </div>
      {count > 0 && (
        <div class="dc-history" data-group="history">
          <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary" disabled={!current.canUndo} onClick={() => act(editor => editor.undo())}>
            <Icon name="undo" /><span>{translator.t('undo')}</span>
          </button>
          <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary" disabled={!current.canRedo} onClick={() => act(editor => editor.redo())}>
            <Icon name="redo" /><span>{translator.t('redo')}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {count === 0 && open?.kind !== 'add' && <p class="dc-empty" data-body>{translator.t('noFigures')}</p>}
      {(count > 0 || open?.kind === 'add') && (
        <div ref={list} class="dc-list dc-figures" role="list" data-list data-dragging={drag.view ? '' : undefined}>
          {routine.figures.map((figure, index) => {
            const isOpen = open?.kind === 'edit' && open.index === index;
            return (
              <div key={figure.id} role="listitem" data-index={index} data-row data-open={isOpen ? '' : undefined}
                class={`dc-row${sounding(index) ? ' dc-row--now' : ''}${drag.view?.from === index ? ' dc-row--dragging' : ''}`}>
                <div class="dc-row-line">
                  <button type="button" class="dc-row-head dc-figure-head" aria-expanded={isOpen ? 'true' : 'false'}
                    onClick={() => act(editor => (isOpen ? editor.close() : editor.openRow(index)))}>
                    <span class="dc-figure-no">{index + 1}</span>
                    <span class="dc-row-text">
                      <b>{figureDisplayName(routine.danceId, figure.name, locale)}</b>
                      <small>{formatCounts(routine.danceId, figure.counts)}</small>
                    </span>
                    <Icon name="chevron" class="dc-row-chev" />
                  </button>
                  {count > 1 && (
                    <button type="button" class="dc-grip" aria-keyshortcuts="ArrowUp ArrowDown"
                      aria-label={translator.t('moveFigureLabel', { name: figureDisplayName(routine.danceId, figure.name, locale) })}
                      {...drag.gripProps(index)}>
                      <Icon name="grip" /><span aria-hidden="true">{translator.t('move')}</span>
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
                {isOpen && open.kind === 'edit' && (
                  <div class="dc-row-body" data-row-body>
                    <FigureForm idPrefix={`dc-f${index}`} danceId={routine.danceId} draft={open.draft} check={current.check()} adding={false}
                      onDraft={patch => { current.setDraft(patch); rerender(0); }} onDecide={() => act(editor => editor.decide())} />
                    <div class="dc-figure-actions" data-group="figure-actions">
                      <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary dc-span3" disabled={!current.previewOpen()} onClick={previewOpen}>
                        <Icon name={playing ? 'stop' : 'play'} /><span>{playing ? translator.t('stop') : translator.t('previewFigure')}</span>
                      </button>
                      <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary" disabled={index === 0} onClick={() => act(editor => editor.moveOpen(-1))}>
                        <Icon name="up" /><span>{translator.t('moveUp')}</span>
                      </button>
                      <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary" disabled={index === count - 1} onClick={() => act(editor => editor.moveOpen(1))}>
                        <Icon name="down" /><span>{translator.t('moveDown')}</span>
                      </button>
                      <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary" disabled={!current.canAdd} onClick={() => act(editor => editor.duplicateOpen())}>
                        <Icon name="copy" /><span>{translator.t('duplicate')}</span>
                      </button>
                      <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary dc-span3" onClick={() => act(editor => editor.removeOpen())}>
                        <Icon name="trash" /><span>{translator.t('delete')}</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {drag.view?.lineTop != null && <div class="dc-drop-line" style={{ top: `${drag.view.lineTop - 1.5}px` }} aria-hidden="true" />}
          {open?.kind === 'add' && (
            <div role="listitem" class={`dc-row${sounding(count) ? ' dc-row--now' : ''}`} data-row data-open="">
              <div class="dc-row-head dc-figure-head">
                <span class="dc-figure-no">{count + 1}</span>
                <span class="dc-row-text"><b>{translator.t('newFigure')}</b></span>
              </div>
              <div class="dc-row-body" data-row-body>
                <FigureForm idPrefix="dc-fnew" danceId={routine.danceId} draft={open.draft} check={current.check()} adding
                  onDraft={patch => { current.setDraft(patch); rerender(0); }} onDecide={() => act(editor => editor.decide())} />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {count > 0 && open?.kind !== 'add' && (
        <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary dc-wide dc-add-tail" disabled={!current.canAdd} onClick={() => act(editor => editor.openAdd())}>
          <Icon name="plus" /><span>{translator.t('addFigure')}</span>
        </button>
      )}
      <MoreOptions label={translator.t('moreOptions')} open={current.moreOpen} onToggle={next => { if (next !== current.moreOpen) act(editor => editor.setMoreOpen(next)); }}>
        <div>
          <div class="dc-field-label" id="dc-s4-leadin-label">{translator.t('leadInLength')}</div>
          <div class="dc-chips" role="radiogroup" aria-labelledby="dc-s4-leadin-label" data-group="lead-in-bars">
            {([1, 2] as const).map(bars => (
              <button key={bars} type="button" class="dc-chip" role="radio" aria-checked={routine.countInBars === bars ? 'true' : 'false'}
                onClick={() => act(editor => editor.setLeadInBars(bars))}>
                {translator.t(bars === 1 ? 'oneBar' : 'twoBars')}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <RoutineSpeedField profile={profile} bpm={routine.bpm} onChange={bpm => act(editor => editor.setBpm(bpm))} />
      </MoreOptions>
      {!(count === 0 && open?.kind === 'add') && (
        <div class="dc-dock">
          {count === 0
            ? <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-primary dc-dock-button" onClick={() => act(editor => editor.openAdd())}>
                <Icon name="plus" /><span>{translator.t('addFigure')}</span>
              </button>
            : <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-primary dc-dock-button" onClick={previewAll}>
                <Icon name={playing ? 'stop' : 'play'} /><span>{playing ? translator.t('stop') : translator.t('previewAll')}</span>
              </button>}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

// 名前（常に表示の入力欄）。確定は blur か Enter。空なら直前の名前に戻す（Ruling 36-6）。
function RoutineNameField({ name, onCommit }: { name: string; onCommit(name: string): void }) {
  const { translator } = useI18n();
  const [text, setText] = useState(name);
  useEffect(() => setText(name), [name]);
  const commit = () => { if (text.trim() === '') setText(name); else onCommit(text); };
  return (
    <div class="dc-field">
      <div class="dc-field-label" id="dc-s4-name-label">{translator.t('routineName')}</div>
      <input class="dc-input" type="text" aria-labelledby="dc-s4-name-label" value={text} maxLength={ROUTINE_NAME_MAX} autocomplete="off"
        onInput={event => setText((event.currentTarget as HTMLInputElement).value)} onBlur={commit}
        onKeyDown={event => { if (event.key === 'Enter') (event.currentTarget as HTMLInputElement).blur(); }} />
    </div>
  );
}

// 速さ指定（くわしく）: 「種目の標準」とスライダーで1グループ。標準と同じ値は空（種目の標準）として持つ。
function RoutineSpeedField({ profile, bpm, onChange }: { profile: SpeedProfile; bpm: number | null; onChange(bpm: number | null): void }) {
  const { translator } = useI18n();
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const shown = drag ?? bpm ?? profile.practiceBpm;
  const commit = (value: unknown) => {
    const next = clampBpm(value);
    if (next !== null) onChange(next === profile.practiceBpm ? null : next);
  };
  return (
    <div class="dc-fine" data-group="routine-speed">
      <div class="dc-field-label" id="dc-s4-speed-label">
        <span>{translator.t('routineSpeed')}</span>
        <span>{translator.t('tempoMark', { tempo: shown })}</span>
      </div>
      <input aria-labelledby="dc-s4-speed-label" type="range" min={BPM_MIN} max={BPM_MAX} step={1} value={shown}
        onInput={event => setDrag(Number((event.currentTarget as HTMLInputElement).value))}
        onChange={event => { setDrag(null); commit((event.currentTarget as HTMLInputElement).value); }} />
      <button type="button" class="dc-chip" role="radio" aria-checked={bpm === null ? 'true' : 'false'} onClick={() => onChange(null)}>
        {translator.t('danceStandardSpeed', { tempo: profile.practiceBpm })}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

App（`git apply` で当てる。当たらない時は手で直さず、止めて報告する）:
```bash
git apply --whitespace=nowarn <<'PATCH'
diff --git a/src/app/App.tsx b/src/app/App.tsx
index 7fe8ce9..1504c88 100644
--- a/src/app/App.tsx
+++ b/src/app/App.tsx
@@ -11,6 +11,7 @@ import { resolveScreen } from './router/resolve.ts';
 import { buildHash, showsTabs, tabOf, type ScreenId } from './router/routes.ts';
 import { HomeScreen } from './screens/HomeScreen.tsx';
 import { PracticeScreen } from './screens/PracticeScreen.tsx';
+import { RoutineEditScreen } from './screens/RoutineEditScreen.tsx';
 import { RoutinesScreen } from './screens/RoutinesScreen.tsx';
 import { SettingsScreen } from './screens/SettingsScreen.tsx';
 import { SoundHelpScreen } from './screens/SoundHelpScreen.tsx';
@@ -113,6 +114,7 @@ export function App() {
             <IconSprite />
             {screen === 'S2' ? <PracticeScreen key={route.params.danceId} danceId={route.params.danceId!} uiState={route.query.uiState} />
               : screen === 'S3' ? <RoutinesScreen uiState={route.query.uiState} />
+              : screen === 'S4' ? <RoutineEditScreen routineId={route.params.routineId} />
               : <Simple />}
             {showsTabs(screen) && <TabBar current={tabOf(screen)} />}
           </div>
PATCH
```

見た目（S4・つかみ・D10）（`git apply` で当てる。当たらない時は手で直さず、止めて報告する）:
```bash
git apply --whitespace=nowarn <<'PATCH'
diff --git a/src/app/styles/base.css b/src/app/styles/base.css
index 4feb482..1f1891c 100644
--- a/src/app/styles/base.css
+++ b/src/app/styles/base.css
@@ -7,6 +7,7 @@
   --line-on-soft: #7D8699; /* --soft の上の罫線・枠（3.21:1。白の上の --line と同じ強さ。U7） */
   --sel: #1B2A4A; --sel-ink: #FFFFFF; --sel-line: #1B2A4A;
   --count-ink: #A54D06; --beat-on: #A54D06;
+  --now-bg: #FBEBDD; /* 鳴っているフィガーの行の背景だけ（D10） */
   --warn-bg: #FFFFFF; --warn-ink: #7A3A05; --warn-line: #A54D06;
   --icon-circle: #14213D;
   --r: 6px; --r-s: 4px; --bw: 1px; --tabh: 68px; --tabh-top: 64px; --dockh: 97px; --track: -0.015em;
@@ -161,6 +162,47 @@ button { cursor: pointer; }
 .dc-dialog-buttons { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 12px; }
 .dc-dialog-buttons .dc-button { min-height: 56px; }
 
+/* S4 ルーティン編集（Ruling 36〜39・D10） */
+.dc-focus-head { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; }
+.dc-focus-title { font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; }
+.dc-tiles button.dc-tile { border: none; border-bottom: var(--bw) solid var(--line); background: var(--surface); color: var(--ink); text-align: left; }
+.dc-field { margin-top: 12px; }
+.dc-field > .dc-field-label { margin: 0 0 6px; }
+.dc-input { width: 100%; min-height: 52px; padding: 0 12px; border: var(--bw) solid var(--line); border-radius: var(--r-s); background: var(--surface);
+  color: var(--ink); font: inherit; font-size: 18px; }
+.dc-input--counts { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; letter-spacing: 0.04em; }
+.dc-input--bad { border: 2px solid var(--warn-line); }
+.dc-reason { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; margin-top: 6px; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--warn-ink); }
+.dc-suggestions { display: flex; flex-direction: column; margin-top: 6px; border: var(--bw) solid var(--line); border-radius: var(--r-s); background: var(--surface); }
+.dc-suggestion { min-height: 48px; padding: 0 12px; border: none; border-bottom: var(--bw) solid var(--soft); background: none; text-align: left; font-size: 18px; color: var(--ink); }
+.dc-suggestion:last-child { border-bottom: none; }
+.dc-dance-line { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 8px; min-height: 56px; margin-top: 8px; border-bottom: var(--bw) solid var(--line); font-size: 18px; }
+.dc-history { display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 12px; }
+.dc-history .dc-button { flex: 1; }
+.dc-figures { margin-top: 12px; }
+.dc-figure-head { min-height: 56px; padding: 6px 2px 6px 12px; }  /* 鳴っている行の左線（4px）と番号が重ならない */
+.dc-figure-no { flex: none; width: 28px; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--ink2); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
+.dc-row--now { background: var(--now-bg); box-shadow: inset 4px 0 0 var(--beat-on); }  /* 鳴っているフィガー（D10） */
+.dc-row--now .dc-row-text b { font-weight: 800; }
+/* ドラッグ（Ruling 49）: 行の右端のつかみ「⠿ 動かす」。touch-action:none はつかみだけ（行の上では普通にスクロール）。動きは付けない */
+.dc-row-line { display: flex; align-items: stretch; gap: 10px; }
+.dc-row-line > .dc-row-head { flex: 1; min-width: 0; }
+.dc-grip { flex: none; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 2px; width: 56px; min-height: 48px;
+  padding: 0; border: none; background: none; color: var(--ink2); font-size: 16px; font-weight: 600; line-height: 1.2;
+  touch-action: none; user-select: none; -webkit-user-select: none; -webkit-touch-callout: none; cursor: grab; }
+.dc-figures { position: relative; }
+.dc-figures[data-dragging] { user-select: none; -webkit-user-select: none; cursor: grabbing; }
+.dc-figures[data-dragging] .dc-grip { cursor: grabbing; }
+.dc-row--dragging { outline: 2px solid var(--primary); outline-offset: -2px; }
+.dc-drop-line { position: absolute; left: 0; right: 0; height: 3px; border-radius: 2px; background: var(--primary); pointer-events: none; }
+.dc-figure-form .dc-field:first-child { margin-top: 0; }
+.dc-figure-form > .dc-button { margin-top: 12px; }
+.dc-figure-actions { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 8px; margin-top: 12px; padding-top: 12px; border-top: var(--bw) solid var(--line); }
+.dc-figure-actions .dc-button { padding: 0 6px; }
+.dc-span3 { grid-column: span 3; }
+.dc-add-tail { margin-top: 12px; }
+.dc-dock-button { width: 100%; max-width: 608px; }
+
```

- [ ] **Step 6: 新規の流れとドラッグを手で通す**（`npm run dev`、375×667）

1. `#/routines` →「＋ 新しいルーティン」→ 種目10個（S1 と同じ並び・副題）。ワルツを選ぶ → 名前「ワルツのルーティン」・「種目: ワルツ」と「変える」・「フィガーはまだありません。」・下固定「＋ フィガーを追加」。**ハッシュは `#/routines/new` のまま**（まだ保存しない）。
2. 下固定「＋ フィガーを追加」→ 行が開き、下固定の帯は消える。名前に「ウィ」→ 候補「ウィスク」「バックウィスク」「ウィーブ フロム PP」。カウントに全角で `１２ａ３` → 赤枠と「使える記号: 1 2 3 &」、「追加する」は押せない。
3. カウントを `１２＆３` に、候補の「ウィスク」をタップ → 候補が消える →「追加する」→ **ハッシュが `#/routines/r-…/edit` に置き換わる**（戻るで `#/routines/new` に戻らない）。行に「ウィスク 1 2 & 3」、元に戻す／やり直す、末尾「＋ フィガーを追加」、下固定「全体を試聴」。フィガーが1件の間はつかみ（「⠿ 動かす」）は出ない。
4. あと2つ足す（3件）。各行の右端に「⠿ 動かす」。3行目のつかみをマウスで押したまま先頭より上へ引く → 引いている間は3行目にネイビーの枠、行き先にネイビーの線 → 離すと先頭へ。「元に戻す」で戻る。つかみを軽く押す（動かさない）→ その行が開く。つかみにフォーカスして ↑ ↓ → 1つずつ動き、フォーカスがついて行く。
5. 「全体を試聴」→ はじめの合図の後、鳴っている行が琥珀のうす塗り＋左線（D10）、下固定は「停止」。1回で自動で止まる。鳴っている間につかみを引き始めると音が止まる。
6. 行を開いて「上へ」「下へ」「複製」（名前はそのまま）「削除」（確認なし）→「元に戻す」で戻る。
7. 「戻る」→ S3 に「ワルツのルーティン ワルツ · 3 フィガー」。再読み込みしても残る（並べ替えた順のまま）。行を開くと ▶ 再生・編集・削除（開いた行はうすいグレーの背景）。
8. 削除 → 確認ダイアログ（「やめる」が左・最初にフォーカス）→「削除する」→ 0件。

開発サーバーを止め、自動操作ブラウザを閉じる。

`src/app/screens/RoutinePlayScreen.tsx`（新規・全文）:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { getDanceProfile } from '../../engine/dance-profiles.js';
import { figureDisplayName } from '../../engine/figure-terminology.js';
import { BackButton } from '../components/BackButton.tsx';
import { Icon } from '../components/Icon.tsx';
import { danceName } from '../i18n/index.ts';
import { displayForVoiceKey, formatCounts } from '../lib/count-display.ts';
import { beatIndexInBar } from '../practice/beat-indicator.ts';
import { CountBox, FORCED_NOTICE, FineSpeed, MoreOptions, NoticeBox, PlayDock, PracticeToggles, RepeatRow, SpeedChoices, VoiceChip } from '../practice/controls.tsx';
import { chooseRoutineCustomBpm, chooseRoutinePreset, routineLeadInBars, routinePracticeChoice } from '../practice/routine-preferences.ts';
import type { PracticeOptions, SessionNotice } from '../practice/session.ts';
import type { SpeedProfile } from '../practice/speed.ts';
import { usePracticeSession } from '../practice/use-session.ts';
import type { UiState } from '../router/routes.ts';
import type { Routine } from '../routines/routine.ts';
import type { AppSettings } from '../settings/settings.ts';
import { takeAutoplay } from '../state/autoplay.ts';
import { useI18n } from '../state/i18n.ts';
import { useRoutines } from '../state/routines.ts';
import { useSettings } from '../state/settings.ts';

type Profile = SpeedProfile & { beatsPerBar: number };
interface Range { startIndex: number; endIndex: number }

// S5 ルーティン再生（仕様 §4・Ruling 40・42・46〜48・D10）。S2 と同じ骨格（主要操作も同じ5つ）を practice/controls.tsx で組む。
// 速さの記憶はルーティンごと、トグルと繰り返しは S2 と共通の設定。範囲は記憶しない（開くたびに全体）。
// 鳴っているフィガーは onFrame の event.figureIndex だけから（第2の時間計算をしない）。
export function RoutinePlayScreen({ routineId, uiState }: { routineId: string; uiState?: UiState }) {
  const { routines } = useRoutines();
  const routine = routines.find(item => item.id === routineId);
  // router/resolve.ts が「ある・鳴らせる」を確かめてから来る。消えた直後の1回の描画だけ何も出さない。
  return routine ? <RoutinePlayer routine={routine} uiState={uiState} /> : null;
}

function RoutinePlayer({ routine, uiState }: { routine: Routine; uiState?: UiState }) {
  const { translator } = useI18n();
  const { settings, update } = useSettings();
  const locale = translator.locale;
  const profile = getDanceProfile(routine.danceId) as Profile;
  const choice = routinePracticeChoice(profile, routine, settings);
  const { session, view } = usePracticeSession(routine.id);
  const last = routine.figures.length - 1;
  const [range, setRange] = useState<Range>({ startIndex: 0, endIndex: last });
  const latest = useRef({ settings, view, range });
  latest.current = { settings, view, range };
  const [forcedDismissed, setForcedDismissed] = useState(false);
  const [dragBpm, setDragBpm] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const list = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const optionsFrom = (next: AppSettings, nextRange: Range): PracticeOptions => {
    const nextChoice = routinePracticeChoice(profile, routine, next);
    const whole = nextRange.startIndex === 0 && nextRange.endIndex === last;
    return {
      kind: 'routine', danceId: routine.danceId, figures: routine.figures, ...(whole ? {} : { range: nextRange }),
      bpm: nextChoice.bpm, metronome: next.metronome, countInBars: routineLeadInBars(next, routine), loop: next.loop, track: null
    };
  };

  // S3 の ▶ から来た時だけすぐ鳴らす（一回限りの印。Ruling 32・40）。止めるのは usePracticeSession。
  useEffect(() => {
    if (takeAutoplay({ kind: 'routine', id: routine.id })) {
      try { void session.play(optionsFrom(settings, range)); } catch { /* resolve 済みのルーティン。万一でも止まったまま */ }
    }
  }, [session]);

  // 鳴っている行を一覧の中で見える位置へ。一覧が画面に少しでも見えている時だけ（見ていない時に画面を動かさない。Ruling 46）。
  useEffect(() => {
    const element = list.current;
    if (!element || view.figureIndex === null) return;
    const box = element.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (box.bottom <= 0 || box.top >= document.documentElement.clientHeight) return;
    element.children[view.figureIndex]?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
  }, [view.figureIndex]);

  const isPlaying = () => latest.current.view.playing || latest.current.view.starting;
  const current = () => latest.current.settings;

  const restart = (next: AppSettings, nextRange: Range) => { if (isPlaying()) void session.play(optionsFrom(next, nextRange)); };

  const change = (patch: Partial<AppSettings>) => {
    if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) return;
    const next = { ...latest.current.settings, ...patch };
    latest.current.settings = next;
    update(patch);
    restart(next, latest.current.range);
  };

  const changeRange = (next: Range) => {
    latest.current.range = next;
    setRange(next);
    restart(latest.current.settings, next);
  };

  const togglePlay = () => {
    setForcedDismissed(true);
    if (isPlaying()) session.stop();
    else void session.play(optionsFrom(latest.current.settings, latest.current.range));
  };

  const forced = uiState ? FORCED_NOTICE[uiState] : undefined;
  const notice: SessionNotice = forced && !forcedDismissed ? forced : view.notice;
  const beat = view.playing && !view.countIn ? beatIndexInBar(view.originalTick, profile.beatsPerBar) : null;
  const nowText = view.playing && !view.countIn ? displayForVoiceKey(view.voiceKey) : '';
  const sounding = view.playing && !view.countIn && view.figureIndex !== null ? view.figureIndex : null;
  const shownIndex = sounding ?? range.startIndex;
  const shownFigure = routine.figures[shownIndex]!;
  const idleText = view.playing ? '' : formatCounts(routine.danceId, routine.figures[range.startIndex]!.counts).split(' ')[0] ?? '';
  const playing = view.playing || view.starting;
  const dance = danceName(routine.danceId, locale);
  const subtitle = routine.receivedFrom
    ? translator.t('madeBy', { dance, name: routine.receivedFrom.distributor })
    : translator.t('madeByYou', { dance });
  const figureName = (name: string) => figureDisplayName(routine.danceId, name, locale);

  return (
    <main class="dc-screen dc-screen--focus dc-screen--dock dc-practice dc-routine-play" data-screen="S5" data-playing={playing ? 'true' : 'false'}>
      <BackButton screen="S5" />
      <div class="dc-practice-grid">
        <div class="dc-area-main">
          <header class="dc-playhead">
            <h1 data-ellipsis title={routine.name} aria-label={routine.name}>{routine.name}</h1>
            <small data-ellipsis>{subtitle}</small>
          </header>
          <NoticeBox notice={notice} onClose={() => { setForcedDismissed(true); session.dismissNotice(); }} />
          <CountBox playing={view.playing} countIn={view.countIn} nowText={nowText} idleText={idleText} beatsPerBar={profile.beatsPerBar} beat={beat}>
            <div class={`dc-figure-now${sounding !== null ? ' dc-figure-now--on' : ''}`} data-figure-now data-ellipsis>
              <span class="dc-figure-now-pos">{shownIndex + 1} / {routine.figures.length}</span> {figureName(shownFigure.name)}
            </div>
          </CountBox>
        </div>
        <div class="dc-area-controls">
          <SpeedChoices presets={choice.presets} selected={choice.speed} onChoose={preset => change(chooseRoutinePreset(current(), routine.id, preset))} />
          <PracticeToggles metronome={settings.metronome} leadIn={settings.countInBars === 1}
            onMetronome={() => change({ metronome: !current().metronome })}
            onLeadIn={() => change({ countInBars: current().countInBars === 1 ? 0 : 1 })} />
          <VoiceChip />
          <MoreOptions label={choice.customBpm !== null ? translator.t('moreOptionsWithTempo', { tempo: choice.customBpm }) : translator.t('moreOptions')}>
            <div>
              <div class="dc-field-label">{translator.t('practiceRange')}</div>
              <div class="dc-range" data-group="range">
                <RangeStep label={translator.t('rangeStart')} index={range.startIndex} name={figureName(routine.figures[range.startIndex]!.name)}
                  canPrev={range.startIndex > 0} canNext={range.startIndex < last}
                  onStep={delta => { const start = range.startIndex + delta; changeRange({ startIndex: start, endIndex: Math.max(range.endIndex, start) }); }} />
                <RangeStep label={translator.t('rangeEnd')} index={range.endIndex} name={figureName(routine.figures[range.endIndex]!.name)}
                  canPrev={range.endIndex > 0} canNext={range.endIndex < last}
                  onStep={delta => { const end = range.endIndex + delta; changeRange({ startIndex: Math.min(range.startIndex, end), endIndex: end }); }} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <FineSpeed idPrefix="dc-s5" bpm={choice.bpm} dragBpm={dragBpm} competition={profile.competitionBpm} onDrag={setDragBpm}
              onCommit={value => change(chooseRoutineCustomBpm(current(), profile, routine, value))} />
            <RepeatRow loop={settings.loop} onToggle={() => change({ loop: !current().loop })} />
          </MoreOptions>
        </div>
        <div class="dc-area-list">
          <h2 class="dc-list-heading">{translator.t('figuresHeading')}</h2>
          <div ref={list} class="dc-list dc-play-list" role="list" data-list>
            {routine.figures.map((figure, index) => {
              const outside = index < range.startIndex || index > range.endIndex;
              return (
                <div key={figure.id} role="listitem" class={`dc-play-row${sounding === index ? ' dc-row--now' : ''}${outside ? ' dc-play-row--out' : ''}`}>
                  <span class="dc-figure-no">{index + 1}</span>
                  <span class="dc-play-name">{figureName(figure.name)}</span>
                  <small>{formatCounts(routine.danceId, figure.counts)}</small>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <PlayDock playing={playing} onToggle={togglePlay} />
    </main>
  );
}

// 範囲の段送り（Ruling 40）。アイコン単独は禁止なので「‹ 前」「次 ›」と文字を添える（仕様 §5）。
function RangeStep({ label, index, name, canPrev, canNext, onStep }: {
  label: string; index: number; name: string; canPrev: boolean; canNext: boolean; onStep(delta: -1 | 1): void;
}) {
  const { translator } = useI18n();
  return (
    <div class="dc-range-step">
      <span class="dc-range-label">{label}</span>
      <span class="dc-range-value">{index + 1} {name}</span>
      <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary dc-step" disabled={!canPrev} onClick={() => onStep(-1)}>
        <Icon name="chev-l" /><span>{translator.t('previous')}</span>
      </button>
      <button type="button" class="dc-button dc-button--secondary dc-step" disabled={!canNext} onClick={() => onStep(1)}>
        <span>{translator.t('next')}</span><Icon name="chev-r" />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```bash
+   見出し（ルーティン名）は1行で省略・副題は常に2行目、現在カウント 72px、余白 8→4・12→8（タップ間隔 8px は保つ） */
+.dc-routine-play .dc-playhead { gap: 0 12px; }

+.dc-figure-now--on { border-left-color: var(--beat-on); color: var(--ink); }

+.dc-range-label { font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--ink2); }

+.dc-step { padding: 0 8px; gap: 2px; }

+.dc-play-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; min-height: 48px; padding: 4px 2px 4px 12px; border-bottom: var(--bw) solid var(--line); font-size: 18px; }

+
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 手で通す**（`npm run dev`）

1. 375×667: Task 9 の手順でワルツのルーティン（フィガー3つ以上）を作り、S3 で行を開いて「▶ 再生」→ **S5 に着いてすぐ鳴る**（タブ → 行 → ▶ の3タップ目）。
2. 見出しはルーティン名（長ければ1行で「…」）、副題「ワルツ · あなたが作成」。大きなカウントは 72px、拍の下に「1 / 3 ＜名前＞」の1行（鳴っている間は琥珀の左線）。速さ3択・トグル2つ・声チップ・▶ の帯が**スクロールなしで全部見える**。開発者ツールで `document.querySelector('.dc-dock').getBoundingClientRect().top - document.querySelector('.dc-chip-row').getBoundingClientRect().bottom` が 8 以上（Ruling 47）。
3. 「くわしく」→「練習する範囲」の「はじめ」「おわり」を「‹ 前」「次 ›」で動かす（はじめ ≦ おわりを保つ・端のボタンは押せない）。範囲の外の行は灰色。鳴っている最中に変えると、止まって頭から（範囲のはじめから）鳴り直す。
4. ページの下の「フィガー」一覧が画面に見えている時だけ、鳴っている行が見える位置へ動く。**一覧が画面の外の時はページが動かない**（発見 #1）。
5. 1280×900: 左にカウントと一覧、右に操作。はじめの合図の間は「はじめの合図…」が 32px（200px にならない。発見 #7）。
6. 戻る → S3。S5 を再読み込みしても勝手に鳴らない。`#/routines/new/play`・存在しない ID・フィガー0件のルーティンの `…/play` は S3 に置き換わる。

### Task 11: ui:check を S3〜S5 へ広げる・段3のスモーク

**Files:**
- Create: `tools/ui-check-routines.js`（検査用ルーティン。カウントは templates、名前は verified 名。40字の名前2本と40字のフィガー名1つ。`seedRoutinesInPage` はブラウザの中で IndexedDB に直接書く）
- Modify: `tools/check-app-ui.js`（23状態・`[data-list]` の数え方と入れ子禁止・`[data-ellipsis]` の例外・R47 の余裕と見出し1行・R44 のボタン順・R36/38 の不正カウント・`DC_ONLY`）
- Create: `tools/smoke-routines.js`／Modify: `package.json`（`smoke:routines`）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 画面の DOM の約束（Task 8〜10 の Produces）
- Produces: `npm run ui:check`（既定 5173、`DC_BASE_URL`、`DC_ONLY=S5,S4` で状態名の頭で絞れる）・`npm run smoke:routines`

- [ ] **Step 1: 検査用データ・ui:check・スモーク**

`tools/ui-check-routines.js`（新規・全文）:
```js
// ui:check と smoke:routines が使う検査用のルーティン（Ruling 43）。
// カウントは種目の templates だけで組み、名前は figure-terminology の照合済みの語だけを使う
// （未監修の旧ルーティンのカウント列を新アプリの経路に流さない）。
import { DANCE_PROFILES } from '../src/engine/dance-profiles.js';
import { FIGURE_TERMINOLOGY } from '../src/engine/figure-terminology.js';

const CREATED = '2026-10-11T08:00:00.000Z';

function verifiedNames(danceId) {
  const names = [];
  for (const entry of Object.values(FIGURE_TERMINOLOGY)) {
    if (entry.danceId === danceId && entry.claimStatus === 'verified' && !names.includes(entry.sourceAliases[0])) names.push(entry.sourceAliases[0]);
  }
  return names;
}

function routine(id, danceId, name, figureCount, minute, firstFigureName) {
  const names = verifiedNames(danceId);
  const templates = DANCE_PROFILES[danceId].templates;
  const createdAt = CREATED.replace('08:00', `08:${String(minute).padStart(2, '0')}`);
  return {
    id, danceId, name, bpm: null, countInBars: 1, source: 'user-created', status: 'ready',
    figures: Array.from({ length: figureCount }, (_, index) => ({
      id: `f-uicheck-${String(index).padStart(4, '0')}`,
      name: index === 0 && firstFigureName ? firstFigureName : names[index % names.length],
      counts: templates[index % templates.length]
    })),
    provenance: { sourceKind: 'user-created', authority: null, authorityClaimStatus: 'not-applicable', reviewStatus: 'unreviewed' },
    createdWith: { app: 'dance-count', version: 'ui-check' },
    createdAt, updatedAt: createdAt
  };
}

// 40 字ちょうどの名前（Ruling 47: 全角 40 字と英数字 40 字の2本）。
const LONG_JA = 'ワルツの通し練習・金曜クラス発表会に向けて先生と一緒に確認する用の大事なルーティン'.slice(0, 40);
const LONG_EN = 'Friday class waltz run-through for show!'.padEnd(40, 'x').slice(0, 40);
// 先生が自由に打った長いフィガー名（40 字）。名前だけで、カウントは templates のまま（Ruling 3・43）。
const LONG_FIGURE = 'ナチュラルターンからスピンターンへつなぐ先生オリジナルの入り方（金曜クラス用・前半）'.slice(0, 40);

export const UI_CHECK_ROUTINES = {
  waltz13: routine('r-uicheck-waltz13', 'waltz', 'ワルツの練習', 13, 0),
  empty: routine('r-uicheck-empty00', 'tango', 'タンゴの練習', 0, 1),
  longJa: routine('r-uicheck-longja40', 'waltz', LONG_JA, 3, 2, LONG_FIGURE),
  longEn: routine('r-uicheck-longen40', 'jive', LONG_EN, 3, 3)
};

// ブラウザの中で動く（page.evaluate に渡す）。アプリが版上げした DB（dance-count-v2 v2）の routines へ直接書く。
export async function seedRoutinesInPage(routines) {
  const db = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const request = indexedDB.open('dance-count-v2', 2);
    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction('routines', 'readwrite');
    for (const item of routines) transaction.objectStore('routines').put(item);
    transaction.oncomplete = resolve;
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
  });
  db.close();
}
```

```bash
+    const heading = document.querySelector('h1');
+    const lines = heading ? Math.round(heading.getBoundingClientRect().height / parseFloat(getComputedStyle(heading).lineHeight)) : 0;
+    if (lines !== 1) issues.push(`R47 heading is ${lines} lines, expected 1`);
+  }
+  // Ruling 44: 確認ダイアログは質問1つ・ボタン2つ・取り消しが左
+  if (state.confirm) {
+    const dialog = document.querySelector('dialog[open][data-confirm]');
+    if (!dialog) issues.push('R44 confirm dialog is not open');
+    else {
+      const buttons = [...dialog.querySelectorAll('button')].filter(visible);
+      if (buttons.length !== 2) issues.push(`R44 dialog has ${buttons.length} buttons, expected 2`);
+      const [left] = buttons.sort((a, b) => a.getBoundingClientRect().left - b.getBoundingClientRect().left);
+      if (!left?.matches('[data-cancel]')) issues.push('R44 the cancel button is not on the left');
+    }
+  }
+  // Ruling 36・38: 不正なカウントは赤枠と理由の1行、「追加する」は押せない
+  if (state.invalidCounts) {
+    if (!document.querySelector('.dc-input--bad[aria-invalid="true"]')) issues.push('R38 invalid counts are not marked');
+    const reason = document.querySelector('.dc-reason');
+    if (!reason || !visible(reason)) issues.push('R38 the reason line is not shown');
+    const decide = [...document.querySelectorAll('.dc-figure-form .dc-primary')].pop();
+    if (!decide?.disabled) issues.push('R36 "Add" is pressable with invalid counts');
+  }
   return issues;
 }
PATCH
```

`tools/smoke-routines.js`（新規・全文）:
```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// 段3の完了条件（全体地図・Ruling 43）を本物の Chrome で確かめる。
// UI の操作だけで: ワルツのルーティンを作る（種目 → フィガー2本を「追加する」で確定・不正なカウントでは押せない）→
// つかみで引いて並べ替える・元に戻す・再読み込みで残る・軽く押すと行が開く（Ruling 49）→
// 行を開いて書き換えたまま戻る（閉じ方の原則で確定）→
// 再読み込みしても一覧に残る → 行を開いて ▶ → S5 で鳴る（大きなカウント表示が音声の時計に沿って変わる）→ 戻る → 削除 → 0件。
// 前提: `npm run dev`（既定 http://localhost:5173/）。DC_BASE_URL で変更可。失敗があれば終了コード1。
import { chromium } from 'playwright-core';

const BASE = process.env.DC_BASE_URL ?? 'http://localhost:5173/';
const SOUND_TIMEOUT_MS = 10000;   // はじめの合図（1小節）＋声の読み込みを待っても、この間にカウントが3種類変わること
// 画面は先に変わり、端末への書き込みはその少し後に確定する（platform/storage.ts）。確定前に再読み込みすると
// 取り消されるので、再読み込みの前にこれだけ待つ（人が「消した直後」に再読み込みする競り合いはアプリでは防げない）。
const WRITE_SETTLE_MS = 500;
const errors = [];
const browser = await chromium.launch({ channel: 'chrome' });

async function addFigure(page, name, counts) {
  const form = page.locator('.dc-figure-form').last();
  await form.locator('input').nth(0).fill(name);
  await form.locator('input').nth(1).fill(counts);
  await form.locator('.dc-primary').click();
}

async function expectSounding(page, label) {
  const seen = new Set();
  const end = Date.now() + SOUND_TIMEOUT_MS;
  while (Date.now() < end && seen.size < 3) {
    const text = (await page.locator('.dc-now').textContent())?.trim();
    if (text && /^[1-8SQ&a]$/.test(text)) seen.add(text);
    await page.waitForTimeout(100);
  }
  if (seen.size < 3) errors.push(`${label}: count display did not advance (${[...seen].join(', ')})`);
  if ((await page.locator('[data-alert]').getAttribute('data-alert').catch(() => null)) === 'no-sound') errors.push(`${label}: no-sound banner shown`);
}

try {
  const context = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: 375, height: 667 }, locale: 'ja-JP' });
  const page = await context.newPage();
  page.on('pageerror', error => errors.push(`pageerror: ${error.message}`));
  page.on('console', message => { if (message.type() === 'error') errors.push(`console: ${message.text()}`); });

  await page.goto(`${BASE}#/routines`);
  await page.waitForSelector('[data-screen="S3"]');
  if (await page.locator('[data-row]').count()) errors.push('S3 should start empty');

  // 作る: 新しいルーティン → 種目（ワルツ）→ 0件の下固定「＋ フィガーを追加」
  await page.locator('[data-screen="S3"] .dc-primary').click();
  await page.waitForSelector('[data-screen="S4"] [data-group="dance"]');
  await page.locator('[data-group="dance"] button').first().click();
  if (!page.url().endsWith('#/routines/new')) errors.push(`choosing a dance must not save yet (url ${page.url()})`);
  await page.locator('.dc-dock .dc-primary').click();
  await addFigure(page, 'ナチュラルターン', '１２３');   // 全角でも受け付ける（Ruling 38）
  await page.waitForURL(/#\/routines\/r-[A-Za-z0-9-]+\/edit$/);

  // 2本目: 不正なカウントでは「追加する」が押せない → 直すと押せる
  await page.locator('.dc-add-tail').click();
  const form = page.locator('.dc-figure-form').last();
  await form.locator('input').nth(0).fill('ウィスク');
  await form.locator('input').nth(1).fill('12a3');
  if (!(await form.locator('.dc-primary').isDisabled())) errors.push('"Add" must be disabled for 12a3 in waltz');
  await form.locator('input').nth(1).fill('12&3');
  await form.locator('.dc-primary').click();
  await page.locator('.dc-add-tail').click();
  await addFigure(page, 'クローズドチェンジ', '123');
  const names = () => page.locator('.dc-figure-head b').allTextContents();
  if ((await names()).length !== 3) errors.push(`expected 3 figures, found ${(await names()).length}`);

  // ドラッグ（Ruling 49）: 3行目のつかみを先頭より上へ引く → 並びが変わり、枠と線が消える → 元に戻す → やり直す
  const grip = await page.locator('.dc-grip').nth(2).boundingBox();
  const firstRow = await page.locator('.dc-row[data-index]').first().boundingBox();
  await page.mouse.move(grip.x + grip.width / 2, grip.y + grip.height / 2);
  await page.mouse.down();
  await page.mouse.move(grip.x + grip.width / 2, grip.y - 30, { steps: 3 });
  await page.mouse.move(grip.x + grip.width / 2, firstRow.y - 10, { steps: 3 });
  await page.mouse.up();
  const dragged = ['クローズドチェンジ', 'ナチュラルターン', 'ウィスク'];
  if (JSON.stringify(await names()) !== JSON.stringify(dragged)) errors.push(`drag did not reorder: ${JSON.stringify(await names())}`);
  if (await page.locator('.dc-row--dragging, .dc-drop-line').count()) errors.push('drag marks are left after the drop');
  await page.locator('[data-group="history"] button').first().click();   // 元に戻す
  if ((await names())[0] !== 'ナチュラルターン') errors.push('undo did not restore the order before the drag');
  await page.locator('[data-group="history"] button').last().click();    // やり直す
  await page.waitForTimeout(WRITE_SETTLE_MS);
  await page.reload();
  await page.waitForSelector('.dc-figure-head');
  if (JSON.stringify(await names()) !== JSON.stringify(dragged)) errors.push(`the dragged order did not survive reload: ${JSON.stringify(await names())}`);

  // 4px だけ動かして離す → タップ扱いで行が開く（「動かす」を押して何も起きない状態を作らない）
  const tapGrip = await page.locator('.dc-grip').first().boundingBox();
  await page.mouse.move(tapGrip.x + tapGrip.width / 2, tapGrip.y + tapGrip.height / 2);
  await page.mouse.down();
  await page.mouse.move(tapGrip.x + tapGrip.width / 2, tapGrip.y + tapGrip.height / 2 + 4);
  await page.mouse.up();
  if ((await page.locator('.dc-row[data-index="0"][data-open]').count()) !== 1) errors.push('a short press on the grip should open the row');
  await page.locator('.dc-figure-head').first().click();   // 閉じる

  // 閉じ方の原則（Ruling 36-1）: 行を開いて有効な値を入れたまま「戻る」→ 確定して保存される
  await page.locator('.dc-figure-head').first().click();
  await page.locator('.dc-figure-form input').nth(1).fill('123123');

  // 戻る → 一覧 → 再読み込みしても残る
  await page.locator('[data-back]').click();
  await page.waitForSelector('[data-screen="S3"] [data-row]');
  await page.waitForTimeout(WRITE_SETTLE_MS);
  await page.reload();
  await page.waitForSelector('[data-screen="S3"] [data-row]');
  const rows = await page.locator('[data-row] .dc-row-head').allTextContents();
  if (rows.length !== 1 || !rows[0]?.includes('3 フィガー')) errors.push(`after reload the list shows ${JSON.stringify(rows)}`);
  await page.locator('[data-row] .dc-row-head').first().click();
  await page.locator('[data-row-body] .dc-button--secondary').first().click();   // 編集
  await page.waitForSelector('[data-screen="S4"] .dc-figure-head');
  const firstCounts = (await page.locator('.dc-figure-head small').first().textContent())?.trim();
  if (firstCounts !== '1 2 3 1 2 3') errors.push(`back with a valid open row should commit it (first figure shows "${firstCounts}")`);
  await page.locator('[data-back]').click();
  await page.waitForSelector('[data-screen="S3"]');

  // 鳴らす: 行を開いて ▶（タブ → 行 → ▶ の3タップ目）→ S5 ですぐ鳴る
  await page.locator('[data-row] .dc-row-head').first().click();
  await page.locator('[data-row-body] .dc-primary').click();
  await page.waitForSelector('[data-screen="S5"]');
  await expectSounding(page, 'S5');

  // 消す: 戻る → 行を開いて削除 → 確認の「削除する」→ 0件
  await page.locator('[data-back]').click();
  await page.waitForSelector('[data-screen="S3"]');
  await page.locator('[data-row] .dc-row-head').first().click();
  await page.locator('[data-row-body] .dc-button--secondary').last().click();
  await page.locator('dialog[open] .dc-primary').click();
  await page.waitForSelector('.dc-empty');
  await page.waitForTimeout(WRITE_SETTLE_MS);
  await page.reload();
  await page.waitForSelector('[data-screen="S3"]');
  if (await page.locator('[data-row]').count()) errors.push('the deleted routine came back after reload');
  await context.close();
} finally {
  await browser.close();
}

if (errors.length) {
  console.error(errors.join('\n'));
  process.exit(1);
}
console.log('routines smoke OK: created by taps, invalid counts blocked, dragged and undone, survives reload, plays in S5, deleted');
```

| 15 | 「⠿ 動かす」を軽く押す | その行が開く（「上へ」「下へ」で動かせる） |

## 段3の完了条件（全体地図・仕様 §13）

- 作成 → 保存 → 再読み込みで残る（`smoke:routines`）
- 不正なカウントの赤枠と理由の1行、「追加する／決定」が押せない（`ui:check` の S4 不正カウント・`smoke:routines`）
- `ui:check` 93件 PASS（dev と公開ビルド）。S3＝1/3・S4＝1/3/5/7/7・S5＝5/8（Ruling 35・36・40・43）、S5 の余裕 8px 以上（Ruling 47）
- `npm test` 288件・typecheck・build・timing identical・ゴールデン不変
- フィガーのドラッグ（Ruling 49）: マウスは `smoke:routines`、指は実機確認表の 11〜15
- 実機確認（`docs/app-v1/stage3-device-check.md`）はひとみうさ
